                                           Re: Gibbs Asked About UFOs On C-SPAN
  From: Don Ledger <dledger.nul>
  Date: Fri, 24 Jul 2009 17:08:08 -0300
  Archived: Sat, 25 Jul 2009 07:00:00 -0400
  Subject: Re: Gibbs Asked About UFOs On C-SPAN


  >Source: The Huffington Post
  >http://tinyurl.com/l3chuz
  >07-24-09
  >Gibbs Asked About UFOs On C-SPAN
  >This morning on C-SPAN, White House Press Secretary Gibbs
  >fielded an unusual question about goverment transparency. The
  >questioner wasn't concerned with CIA reports or white house
  >visitor logs. The latest alleged cover-up? UFOs.
  >"My question might seem a little bit silly but it's very serious
  >and I was wondering if you could take it very seriously." Ryan
  >from Seattle said. "Let the American people know the truth about
  >UFOs..."
  >With a hint of a smile Gibbs responded. "Well Ryan I take your
  >question seriously, not simply because of the topic but also
  >because if you're calling from Seattle and it's a little after 7
  >here... it's a little after 4 in the morning there." Gibbs
  >paused. "Look... I have not been briefed on whether or not there
  >are... the existence of UFOs, I haven't talked to the President
  >about this topic."
  The question might have been better posed by asking, "In light
  of numerous reports of a phenomenon seen both in our skies and
  on the ground and reported as Unidentified Objects by airline
  pilots, private pilots and specifically military pilots
  including those of the United States Air Force, and considering
  the fact that these unidentifieds out class our best fighters in
  speed and manueverability; would the White House Press Secretary
  be inclined to ask the President if he still wants to accept the
  claim by the USAF that this phenomenon does not pose a threat to
  national security given the climate of terrorism this country
  is under?
  In other words how can the US government claim the phenomenon is
  no threat when they are helpless to engage or deter these
  objects that seem to operate in American skies with impunity? Is
  the government and it's armed forces not concerned about this
  embarrassing scenario?"

  Don Ledger

  get a question into President Obama's Press Secretary, Robert

  did not demean the question. There will be more opportunities
  like this. When they present, please consider asking Gibbs

  From: Greg Boone <Evolbaby.nul>

  http://tinyurl.com/mjzueu
  It's a brief snippet about that famous roadside landmark called
  the UFO at Hancock's Harbor. It's been sold and will be moving
  to a new location.
  What gets me is this building/structure has grown to landmark
  status and one realizes just how long this UFO thing has been
  going on. It's way past just a pop culture designation, it's
  part of something more considering it's longevity and expansion
  throughout all our media and culture.
  One day it might get up there with the Flag and mom's apple pie.
  I know, I just scared some people.

Is Hotel California About Abductions?




                                                           UFO Updates
                                    A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                                'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2009 > Jul > Jul 26

                                              Is Hotel California About Abductions?
  From: William Sawers <ufsyntax.nul>
  Date: Sun, 26 Jul 2009 16:47:38 +1000
  Archived: Sun, 26 Jul 2009 06:42:08 -0400
  Subject: Is Hotel California About Abductions?

  Hi All,
  I was just checking on a meaning of the Phil Collins song In The
  Air Tonight and came across this interesting meaning, among many
  others for The Eagles song Hotel California. I thought someone
  might find it interesting as well.

  Regards
  William
  -----
  Source: In The 70's.Com
  http://www.inthe70s.com/generated/lyricsmeaning.shtml

  The Eagles' Hotel California
  Although I think many of you have hit upon some very interesting
  ideas about the lyrics here (and justifiable ones at that -
  especially the "in limbo" entries, as that is my 2nd choice
  regarding the meaning), nobody as yet has suggested that the
  song is about an alien abduction (yes, I believe in UFOs).
  [* Note well - the aliens' UFO/planet/people have apparently
  recreated the look of earth and earthlings to gain confidence
  and complicity by the abductees.]
  Here are arguments regarding my theory:
  1) First off, the very opening lines certainly point to an
  abduction - "On a dark desert highway, cool wind in my hair;
  Warm smell of colitis rising up through the air. Up ahead in the
  distance I saw a shimmering light - My head grew heavy, and my
  sight grew dim; I had to stop for the night."
  Dissecting these lines, we find the following:
  2) 'On a dark desert highway' = aliens nearly always take and
  perform their experiments at night, and pick remote areas to do
  so.
  3) 'UP' (ahead...) suggests he saw something 'ABOVE' another
  thing (his car) - i.e., in this case, the "something" being the
  spacecraft.
  4) smell of colitis = some abductees report a strange odor
  emanating from the aliens or in the craft itself (possibly a
  formaldehyde-like substance??).
  5) 'I saw a shimmering light' = well, we all know by now that
  them thar extraterrestrials like to flash their lights when
  flying/landing.
  6) '...head grew heavy...sight grew dim...' = a hypnotic trance
  that aliens subject humans to, so that they do not resist


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m26-002.shtml[11/12/2011 11:16:59]
Is Hotel California About Abductions?

  abduction. Now on to the rest of the song - the alien mentions
  are more implicit than explicit, yet there are still clues:
  7) 'There she stood in the doorway...this could be Heaven or
  this could be Hell...And she showed me the way; There were
  voices down the corridor - I thought I heard them say Welcome
  ...such a lovely place.' = The 'she' in the lines above can be
  looked upon as the (remember, in human guise) leader of the
  abductees in their doomed exile, like the farmer leading lambs
  to the slaughter (illustrated in the fact that "you can never
  leave").
  8) The extraterrestrial's realm will soon take control now, as
  the humans are brainwashed, repeating, like a mantra: Such a
  lovely place, Such a lovely face.
  9) 'And still those voices are calling from far away; Wake you
  up in the middle of the night' = as in a dream/abductions occur
  at night and many victims say they thought it was only a
  nightmare.
  10) This next segment, '...her mind is... (through)...Some dance
  to forget' = is the narrator being lead on/enticed by the woman
  at the doorway, to comfort him before any predictably sinister
  goings on befall him.
  11) 'bring your alibis' = hard to come by any witnesses to
  abductions - since there were nine for the narrator, despair may
  be setting in.
  12)'Welcome to the Hotel California...Were all just prisoners
  here' = welcome to a prison-like existence where there is no
  leaving.
  13) 'They gathered for the feast; they stab it with their steely
  knives, but they just can't kill the beast. Last thing I
  remember I was running for the door' = These lines are highly
  indicative of alien experiments, and this newcomer (not yet
  brainwashed) is still desperate to survive -> the beast/and/to
  leave -> running for the door.
  14) 'We are programmed to receive' = 'programmed' suggests
  something non-human. Hence, an alien. "You can check out any
  time you like But you can never leave." = 'Check out' represents
  a mental suppression of the abduction, yet the abductees' memory
  will 'never leave.'
  Well folks, that's my interpretation (with limbo as close
  runner-up.)

  AJH,
  Aug. 2006

  On another List, a virtual teletype discussion List, I have
  stumbled across a few old messages in the all capitals teletype
  format. I found the story below quite interesting. American
  embassies and consulates collected open source intelligence in
  many areas of interest to the United States Government. Of
  course UFOs are lumped in with space debris, stealth aircraft,
  and the Apollo program as part of this all sources intelligence
  collection.
  I pieced this two section message from various sources on the
  internet, retyping extensive portions and cleaning up the
  message headings and endings. I am hoping you post this to the
  list for its historical context.
  Best to you,
  Terry

  PAGE 03 RUMOUHA1670 UNCLAS
  ITEM 22. [XXXX] A. ON LATE AFTERNON OF 790816 THE EMBASSY HERE
  RECEIVED INFORMATION THAT A STRANGE OBJECT HAD BEEN FOUND ON A
  FARM NEAR SANTA CRUZ, BOLIVIA. SOURCE STATED OBJECT WAS ABOUT 70
  CENTIMETERS IN DIAMETER AND TWO METERS IN CIRCUMFERENCE WITH A
  HOLE IN ONE SIDE AND A METAL SKIN COVERING OF APPROX. ONE-HALF
  INCH THICKNESS. LATER THE OBJECT WAS DESCRIBED AS "ABOUT THREE
  TIMES THE SIZE OF A BASKETBALL." SOURCE STATED HE HAD FILM OF
  THIS OBJECT WHICH HE GAVE TO AN EMBASSY
  OFFICIAL 790817.
  B. ON THE MORNING OF 790817, THE SANTA CRUZ NEWSPAPER "EL
  DEBER" PUBLISHED A FRONT PAGE ARTICLE ON THIS INCIDENT. AN
  UNOFFICIAL TRANSLATION OF THE ARTICLE FOLLOWS: QUOTE - PART OF
  WHAT COULD BE A SATELLITE FELL NEAR COTOCA. A PHENOMENON THAT
  COULD FOCUS THE ATTENTION OF THE WORLD ON BOLIVIA OCCURRED IN
  OUR CAPITAL WHEN A SPHERE WAS DISCOVERED THAT FELL APPROXIMATELY
  SIX DAYS AGO ON A FARM LOCATED 15KM FROM COTOCA. ACCORDING TO
  INFORMATION RECEIVED BY THIS NEWSPAPER FROM THE DIRECTOR OF THE
  AIR FORCE ACADEMY, COL. ARIEL COCA, THE SPHERE IS MADE OF
  SPECIAL LIGHT ALLOY BUT VERY RESISTANT, POSSIBLY PART OF A FUEL
  TANK OR A SATELLITE! THE OBJECT DOES NOT HAVE ANY SIGNS OR MARKS
  THAT COULD IDENTIFY ITS ORIGIN NOR ITS COUNTRY TO WHICH IT
  BELONGS.


  PAGE 04 RUMOUHA1670 UNCLAS
    COL COCA SAID HE WOULD INFORM HIS SUPERIORS OF THIS
  PHENOMENON SO A STUDY CAN BE MADE TO DETERMINE ITS ORIGIN AND TO
  BE ANALYZED IN CASE IT CONTAINS RADIOACTIVE SUBSTANCE. THE
  PEOPLE WHO LIVE IN THIS AREA WHERE THIS BALL OF FIRE (AS THEY
  CALLED IT) FELL, DELAYED IN TELLING THE AUTHORITIES ABOUT THE
  EVENT BECAUSE THEY WERE AFRAID THAT NO ONE WOULD LISTEN. -
   UNQUOTE.
  C. [XXXX] AN ACCOMPANYING PICTURE IN THE NEWSPAPER SHOWS COL
  ROCA, COMMANDER OF THE BOLIVIAN AIR FORCE ACADEMY, EXAMINING A
  ROUND OBJECT THAT REACHES ABOVE HIS KNEES AND APPEARS TO BE
  PERFECTLY ROUND EXCEPT FOR A HOLE (MISSING PORTION OF OUTER
  SKIN) WHICH IS ABOUT NINE INCHES IN DIAMETER.
  RO COMMENTS: [XXXX] WE HAVE MADE NO INQUIRIES NOR REQUEST FOR
  INFORMATION OTHER THAN TO GET THE UNDEVELOPED FILM DISCUSSED
  EARLIER. WE RECEIVED FROM SOURCE A ROLL OF 35MM FILM FOR COLOR

  PHOTOGRAPH WILL BE FORWARDED BY POUCH ON 790820 TO DIA DT-3. THE

  UNQUOTE. AN UNOFFICIAL TRANSLATION OF THE ARTICLE FOLLOWS,
  QUOTE: A SECOND FIRE BALL FELL FROM THE SKY EARLY IN THE MORNING

  OF THE SAME DAY THAT THE FIRST ONE WAS FOUND NEAR COTOCA. THIS
  SECOND ONE WAS FOUND 200 KILOMETERS NORTH OF THE CITY OF SANTA
  CRUZ ON THE FARM OF JUAN SAAVEDRA BY THE CAMPESIONO GONZALO
  MENACHO VIVERAS. THE PLACE IS IN THE AREA OF BUEN RETIRO NEAR
  THE YARACANE RIVER. ACCORDING TO THE INFORMATION GIVEN BY THE
  CAMPESINO, AROUND 12:30 A.M. ON FRIDAY LAST WEEK HE HEARD A LOUD
  WHISTLING SOUND AND SAW A FIRE BALL FOLLOWED BY AN EXPLOSION. HE
  SAID THAT THE NEXT EVENING A SILENT AIRCRAFT THAT HAD THREE
  LIGHTS WAS FLYING OVER THE EXPLOSION AREA. AFTER DAWN ON FRIDAY
  MORNING THE CAMPESINO STARTED LOOKING AROUND THE AREA OF
  THE IMPACT AND FOUND A SPHERE. AS IT WAS NOT HEAVY, HE TOOK IT HOME
  BT
  #1670




  NNNN

  UNCLAS FINAL SECTION OF 02
  WHERE HE KEPT IT UNTIL HIS FRIEND FRAGILE, MENDEZ HURTADO
  LEARNED OF THE OTHER SPHERE IN COTOCA AND PASSED ON INFORMATION
  CONCERNING


  PAGE 02 RUMOUHA1671 UNCLAS
  THIS SECOND SPHERE. THE MYSTERY OF FINDING THESE TWO SPHERES,
  EXACTLY ALIKE, IS THAT ACCORDING TO THE WITNESSES THEY WERE FIRE
  BALLS - THAT IS TO SAY THAT THESE SPHERES BECAME REAL BALLS OF
  FIRE WHEN THEY ENTERED THE ATMOSPHERE BECAUSE OF THE FRICTION
  AND AFTER A HIGH SPEED FALL THEY HIT THE GROUND. HOWEVER, IN THE
  AREA WHERE THEY HAVE BEEN FOUND, THERE WERE NO SIGNS OF THE
  IMPACT AND LOOKS AS THOUGH THE SPHERES LANDED SMOOTHLY. THE
  MATERIAL OF THE SPHERES IS KIND OF FRAGILE, BUT THEY ARE NOT
  DAMAGED (BY IMPACT) AND IN BOTH CASES THEY ONLY SHOW A HOLE (IN
  OUTER SKIN) WITH MELTED EDGES AND AN EXPLOSION (APPARENTLY)
  DESTROYED THE CONTENTS OF THE INTERIOR. GONZALO MENACHO SAID
  THAT WHERE HE FOUND THE SPHERE HE SAW SOME BURNED PLANTS BUT HE
  DID NOT SEE ANY SIGN OF THE IMPACT ON THE SOFT GROUND. THE
  SPHERE FOUND IN THE NORTH OF THE SANTA CRUZ DEPARTMENT WEIGHS
  ABOUT SIX KILOGRAMS WITH A DIAMETER OF ABOUT 80 CENTIMETERS. THE
  OUTSIDE METAL IS SIMILAR TO COPPER, IS KIND OF DARK WITH LIGHT
  SPOTS, AND HAS APPARENTLY BEEN EXPOSED TO VERY HIGH
  TEMPERATURES. THE SPHERE IS MADE BY TWO PIECES PUT TOGETHER
  WITHOUT ANY RIVETS. ON THE TOP OF THE SPHERE YOU CAN SEE A HOLE
  OF IRREGULAR SHAPE AND NEXT TO IT ANOTHER SMALL ROUND HOLE. IN
  BOTH, THE MATERIAL IS MELTED, AT THE OTHER END OF THE SPHERE
  THERE IS A


  PAGE 03 RUMOUHA1671 UNCLAS
  ROUND AREA THAT LOOKS LIKE A CORK IN A BOTTLE HELD IN PLACE BY
  THREE SEMI-MELTED SCREWS. THE SPHERE WAS BROUGHT TO THE CITY OF
  SANTA CRUZ BY NEWSMEN FROM A LOCAL RADIO STATION WHO INFORMED
  THIS NEWSPAPER OF THE DETAILS. THE CAMPESINO WHO FOUND THIS
  SPHERE CLAIMS IT AS HIS PROPERTY. THE SPHERE WAS ON PUBLIC
  DISPLAY ALL AFTERNOON AT THE RADIO STATION UNQUOTE.
  REPORTING OFFICER COMMENT: A PICTURE ACCOMPANYING THE ABOVE
  ARTICLE SHOWS THIS SECOND UNIDENTIFIED SPHERE TO BE PRACTICALLY
  IDENTICAL IN APPEARANCE TO THE FIRST ONE. A COPY OF THIS ARTICLE
  AND PICTURE WERE FORWARDED TO DIA, DT-3 BY STATE DEPARTMENT
  POUCH ON JULY 20, 1979. ALSO INCLUDED IN THE POUCH WERE THE
  FOLLOWING RELATING TO THE FIRST SPHERE 35 MM COLOR PHOTOGRAPHS;
  AN UNDEVELOPED ROLL OF

  From: John Velez <jvelez49.nul>
  Date: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 15:58:34 -0400
  Archived: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 17:44:40 -0400
  Subject: Richard Hall's Abduction Archive
  Hello All,
  I have been informed by the gentleman that is currently handling
  Dick Halls estate, that Dick had requested that the UFO
  abduction related archive he maintained be transferred to me. I
  am deeply honored. I will do my very best to live up to Dick's
  demonstration of trust and confidence in me.
  In tribute to my departed friend and ally, I will carefully
  review the archive and I will post to this List (and to the
  Virtually Strange Network's, Abduction Information Center) any
  and all information that may prove be of value to any
  active/interested researchers, or any serious students of the
  subject.
  I'm certain it will take me a bit of time to digest and evaluate
  the contents of the archive. As soon as I am able to,
  information will begin to flow. Please be patient and bear with
  me.
  Note for Mark Rodeghier at CUFOS: My spouse has instructions to
  transfer Dick Hall's abduction archive to you, (CUFOS) should
  anything untimely happen to me. In any event, the files will
  eventually end up in the possession of Mark Rodeghier and CUFOS.
  I think Dick would have wanted it that way.
  In the meantime, the files, along with the privacy and anonymity of the
  subjects, will be completely safe while in my possession. Dick thought so
  or he would not have left them to me. The archive is in good hands and
  will continue to be, I have seen to it.
  Regards to all,
  John Velez

                                        Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon
  From: Martin Shough <parcellular.nul>
  Date: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 18:45:01 +0100
  Archived: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 18:45:00 -0400
  Subject: Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon

  >From: Katharina Wilson <K_Wilson.nul>

  >Date: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 11:01:18 -0500

  >I received this from Victor Viggiani at ZlandCommunications,
  >Toronto Ontario Canada:
  >Aldrin's Washington Journal statement segment at:
  >http://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bDIXvpjnRws
  >Does anyone - after viewing it - have any comments they would
  >like to share?
  >Katharina
  Apparently Buzz Aldrin isn't too excited about NASA goung back
  to the moon and instead is promoting a manned expedition to set
  up a permanent base in the Mars system. I wish that could happen
  in my lifetime.
  The so-called monolith Buzz tantalises us with is on Phobos. It
  isn't news. Lan Fleming - no stranger to this List - put up an
  interesting web page nearly a decade ago.
  http://www.vgl.org/webfiles/mars/phobos2/phobos2.htm
  It's certainly intriguing Shadow calculations apparently suggest
  an object 28 stories tall, about 2.5 times as high as it is
  wide. Lan cautions that working out the sun elevation is tricky
  because of the extremely eccentric shape of Phobos, and the
  negligible gravity would tend to allow natural vertical
  structures more readily than would Earth's, for example. But
  it's worth a look, whatever it is.
  Is there any more recent analysis available?

  Martin Shough

  <snip>
  If these photos are not hoaxed then they must be among the most
  fascinating UFO shots ever!
  In style they are identical to photos I have seen produced by
  Robbert van den Broeke in crop circles except the others showed
  alien heads or even images of dead people.
  Paranormal researchers will know that there have been psychics
  in the past who could somehow impress images onto photographs
  using the power of their minds.
  So the question must be asked - are these real time photos of
  UFOs or pictures of what Robbert perceives UFOs look like?
  The 'real' UFO argument is strengthened here because the witness
  called Peter claims he physically saw the UFO that was
  photographed.
  But there again, if a psychic can imprint a photo with thoughts,
  why shouldn't he be able to imprint the same image in somebody's
  mind?
  Getting a handle on this UFO subject doesn't get any easier!
  I'm wondering increasingly if we are not so much perceivers of
  reality - but creators of it...

  Dave Haith

  With respect to a further moon mission, Buzz Aldren says at
  about 0:30,"We should go boldly where man has not gone before."
  The statement implies unequivocally that no one landed on the
  moon during the Apollo mission.
  The comment lends credence to Jay Weidner's contention that
  Stanley Kubrick did the photo work to fool us into thinking that
  the astronauts went to the moon. Kubrick made 2001: A Space
  Odyssey in the same time frame, and Weidner thinks he could have
  used it as a cover. He thinks that the Front Screen Projection
  technique was used in both the moon photos and in the movie to
  create the distant landscape background.
  See http://jayweidner.com/AlchemicalKubrickIIa.html
  If this is what happened, the astronauts may not have gone
  beyond earth orbit.

  William

                                                    All In One Films' UFO Releases
  From: David Cherniack <davidc.nul>
  Date: Wed, 29 Jul 2009 08:58:13 -0400
  Archived: Wed, 29 Jul 2009 09:30:00 -0400
  Subject: All In One Films' UFO Releases

  PRESS RELEASE
  Please feel free to post anywhere in virtual space.
  All in One Films, the producers of UFOs: The Secret History, a
  feature documentary considered by many in the know to be the
  best documentary on the subject ever made, announces the
  availability on DVD of:
  UFOs: The Secret History - 23.95 CAD
  UFOs: Addendum 1 - Abductions - 15.95 CAD
  UFOs: Addendum 2 - The ETH and Contact - 11.95 CAD
  (Available individually or as a 3 DVD set for 39.95 CAD.)
  The Moncla Memories - 21.95 CAD
  Please go to our new web site at:
  http://AllinOneFilms.com
  to order these films or any of the ground breaking documentaries
  we have produced for network television. For those who don't
  have the Adobe Flash plug-in installed we highly recommend it
  for an enhanced experience of viewing clips and films on-line.
  Without it the shop location at:
  http://AllinOneFilms.com/
  zen-cart will still function.
  In the future we hope to make these films available via secure
  on-line streaming. For now, though, some of our older films are
  available to view on-line for free including The ET Hypothesis,
  done for the CBC in 1987 - the very first documentary on UFO
  Abductions. We hope to add SkyMagic (1992) soon.
  ---
  UFOs: The Secret History
  Are Flying Saucers real?
  This ground breaking, feature length, documentary traces the
  cultural history of the UFO phenomenon... from the dawn of the
  modern era in the late '40s, through its transformation into one
  of our most pervasive and powerful mythologies.
  For the first time in a documentary film it assembles the
  complete story of how the question of extraterrestrial
  visitation was taken very seriously by Air Force Intelligence
  but quickly became distorted by the public's fascination with
  the possibility. A powerful mythology emerged that was
  manipulated by the intelligence agencies, at first because of
  Cold War security concerns and, more recently, for as yet
  unknown reasons...though the net result has been to marginalize
  a legitimate phenomenon to the outer fringes of the culture.
    101 minutes
  ---


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m29-008.shtml[11/12/2011 11:17:08]
All In One Films' UFO Releases


  UFOs: Addendum 1 - Abductions
  An in depth discussion of the abduction phenomenon... the most
  detailed ever to be put on film. It includes:
    - Historical discussion of the modern phenomeon
      the impact of Hopkins, Jacobs, and Mack
    - Evidence and Methodology
    - Psychological Perspectives
    - Alternate Reality Perspectives
    - An Experiencer's Viewpoint
  Created from extended interviews done for UFOS: The Secret
  History with Jerry Clark, David Jacobs, Eddie Bullard, Travis
  Walton, Mark Rodeghier, Al Harrison, Steven J. Lynn, Stuart
  Appelle, Janet Colli, Ann Druffel, and Will Bueche.
    143 minutes
  ---
  UFOs: Addendum 2 - The ETH and Contact
  Part 1 examines the Extra-terrestrial Hypothesis - the notion
  that some UFO reports are probably explained by extra-
  terrestrial visitation. The topic is covered from historical,
  scientific, and cultural points of view.
  Part 2 looks at why visitation might not include official
  contact...and examines the potential impact on our civilization.
  Strategies for how an advanced culture might initiate contact
  with a less advanced culture are discussed.
  Created from extended interviews done for UFOS: The Secret
  History with Jerry Clark, Wendy Connors, Jan Aldrich, Michael
  Swords, Robert Friend, Mark Rodeghier, Hal Puthoff, Bernie
  Haisch, Bill Hartmann, Eddie Bullard, and Al Harrison.
    44 minutes
  ---
  The Moncla Memories
  Is Gord Heath the reincarnation of the USAF pilot who
  disappeared chasing a UFO over Lake Superior in 1953?
  In an early evening in November, 1953, a USAF jet was scrambled
  to intercept an unknown target over the lake. The pilot, Lt.
  Gene Moncla, his radar operator, and their F-89 fighter, were
  never seen again.
  Gord Heath, a computer systems engineer, has detailed
  recollections of the life of Gene Moncla. The film explores
  Heath's recall, revisiting with him the places where Gene Moncla
  lived, loved, and worked. In the process it explores the depths
  of identity, examining how we give meaning to our lives through
  memory.
  46 minutes

  ---- www.AllinOneFilms.com ----

  From: Jerome Clark <jkclark.nul>
  Date: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 18:14:27 -0400
  Archived: Wed, 29 Jul 2009 10:21:00 -0400
  Subject: Re: Is Hotel California About Abductions?

  >From: Katharina Wilson <K_Wilson.nul>
  >To: <post.nul>
  >Date: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 12:55:59 -0500

  >>From: William Sawers <ufsyntax.nul>
  >>To: UFO UpDates - Toronto <post.nul>
  >>Date: Sun, 26 Jul 2009 16:47:38 +1000
  >>Subject: Is Hotel California About Abductions?

  >On a similar subject: Sean Casteel (UFO Journalist) has written
  >an in-depth article about one of Bob Dylan's songs and how it
  >probably relates to an abduction experience.
  On seeing these words, I looked up the essay in question on the
  Internet.
  I know something - well, a lot, actually - about Dylan's music
  along with the folk-music and poetic traditions which are its
  inspiration. I can assure you that Casteel's piece is not "in
  depth". Sad Eyed Lady of the Lowlands, which Casteel apparently
  believes is some kind of psychic vision of the Roswell incident,
  is about Dylan's first wife Sara Lowndes. He even acknowledged
  as much explicitly in a later song, Sara.
  The lyrics to Sad Eyed Lady are here. See if you can find the
  crashed saucer:
  http://www.bobdylan.com/#/songs/sad-eyed-lady-lowlands
  I do learn, in any event, that Casteel is interested in UFOs and
  he likes Dylan's songs. Well, so do I. Beyond that, Dylan's work
  evinces no interest even in science fiction, much less in UFOs.
  I've seen some tortured interpretations of Dylan lyrics, but
  Casteel's is surely in a class by itself.

  Jerry Clark

  This is bizarre. After questioning the need to spend money
  visiting the moon "again" Aldrin then unequivocally says that,
  instead, "We should go boldly where man has not gone before, fly
  by the comets, visit the asteroids, visit the moon[s] of
  Mars..."
  This seems like a case of paying insufficient attention and
  hearing what you want to hear.

  Martin Shough

  >William
  >This is bizarre. After questioning the need to spend money
  >visiting the moon "again" Aldrin then unequivocally says that,
  >instead, "We should go boldly where man has not gone before, fly

  Martin
  Not so bizarre. He's talking about justifying going to the moon,
  and immediately follows that with "going boldly where man has
  not gone before". It sounds like an afterthought to me. He then
  goes on to talk about visiting asteroids, mars, etc.
  I could say with just as much justification that you are hearing
  what you want to hear. You choose to parse it differently. In
  retrospect, then, my use of the word 'unequivocally' was
  probably a bit too harsh.
  I think plausible deniability may be encouraged in the UFO, crop
  formation, and other controversies on the fringe by those in a
  position to do so. The purpose would be to let each of us decide
  for ourselves what is real. It seems free will is important.
  This video with Aldrin's comment might be another example of
  that, judging from our opposing interpretations. By analogy, was
  it swamp gas (your view), or a UFO (mine)?
  By the way, I don't "want to hear" that the astronauts did not
  go to the moon. I was as excited about this as most people were


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m29-016.shtml[11/12/2011 11:17:12]
Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon

  back then. But there is sufficient ambiguity now that I am less
  certain that the moon landing actually happened, unfortunately.
  NASA's lack of openness doesn't help. For example, the
  photographic record should have been treated like gold, yet it
  seems that some of it was ordered destroyed and higher quality
  video was overwritten. Was this to cover up what actually
  happened? Then there was NASA occasionally talking to the
  astronauts in code, and apparently cutting off the audio feed
  from the moon at critical moments. Was this a charade to impress
  us with the reality of the situation?
  It would not be the only time in recent history that authority
  figures have lied to us about major events.
  William

  While taking a required literature class in college, the
  instructor said that once the material (poem, novel, song,
  whatever) was published, the writer's original message was no
  more important than the interpretation put on it by the
  audience. I had suggested that sometimes a story had no hidden
  meanings, that it was not a metaphor or allegory, but just a
  story. He then said that it wasn't really important what the
  writer had in mind once it was out there for the public to
  interpret.
  Now, before anyone wants to argue this point, let me say this. I
  did not then, nor do I now accept this. It seemed like an
  instructor attempting to justify his existence with some kind of

                                        Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon
  From: Michael Tarbell <mtarbell.nul>
  Date: Wed, 29 Jul 2009 12:23:21 -0600
  Archived: Wed, 29 Jul 2009 16:00:00 -0400
  Subject: Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon

  >From: Martin Shough <parcellular.nul>
  >To: UFO UpDates - Toronto <post.nul>
  >Date: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 18:45:01 +0100
  >Subject: Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon
  <snip>
  >It's certainly intriguing Shadow calculations apparently suggest
  >an object 28 stories tall, about 2.5 times as high as it is
  >wide. Lan cautions that working out the sun elevation is tricky
  >because of the extremely eccentric shape of Phobos, and the
  >negligible gravity would tend to allow natural vertical
  >structures more readily than would Earth's, for example. But
  >it's worth a look, whatever it is.
  Although low gravity would indeed allow such a formation to
  remain stable, my question is, how it could form in the first
  place, in the absence of any volcanic/tectonic activity or
  erosion forces? The only plausible natural origin that comes to
  my mind is an elongated chunk of crater ejecta that happened to
  land, or finally come to rest, end-on.
  There is no scar or gouge mark apparent in the vicinity, which
  suggests it did not tumble or slide to its current position,
  unless that evidence has been buried by subsequent fall(s) of
  finer debris.
  Even if traveling just a few meters per second at impact (any
  faster and it would have escaped Phobos altogether), it seems
  surprising that such an extended object would remain intact,
  unless the surface at impact was very low density and porous,
  and/or the object itself had high strength and integrity (note
  that the mean density of Phobos is less than ~2 gm/cu.cm).
  Unless further analysis indicates an illusion caused by some
  quirk in the local terrain, I would have to agree that this is
  worth a closer look.

  Mike

  Hi, Kevin,
  >>I do learn, in any event, that Casteel is interested in UFOs and
  >>he likes Dylan's songs. Well, so do I. Beyond that, Dylan's work
  >>evinces no interest even in science fiction, much less in UFOs.
  >>I've seen some tortured interpretations of Dylan lyrics, but
  >>Casteel's is surely in a class by itself.

  >While taking a required literature class in college, the
  >instructor said that once the material (poem, novel, song,
  >whatever) was published, the writer's original message was no
  >more important than the interpretation put on it by the
  >audience. I had suggested that sometimes a story had no hidden
  >meanings, that it was not a metaphor or allegory, but just a
  >story. He then said that it wasn't really important what the
  >writer had in mind once it was out there for the public to
  >interpret.
  I have some small experience of this from my life as a
  songwriter. From time to time I come upon wildly conflicting
  interpretations that are far from what I was thinking about when
  I wrote the lyrics. Sometimes, though, they do make sense.
  A widely recorded song was intended, by me, to be about
  homesickness, but one perfectly intelligent listener wrote that
  its theme was the narrator's agonized reflections about
  encroaching Alzheimer's. Another song sparked a dispute about
  whether it celebrates or ridicules small-town life. Yet another
  seems to have as many interpretations as it has listeners. Many
  of these interpretations strike me as interesting and
  defensible, even if not what I had - at least consciously - in
  mind.
  Nonetheless, unlike Casteel's claims about Dylan and UFOs, the
  text in these instances supported these kinds of
  interpretations, and so they could be argued meaningfully. In
  other words, if you're going to make a case for a particular


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m29-019.shtml[11/12/2011 11:17:13]
Re: Is Hotel California About Abductions?

  reading of a literary text, you can't just say anything that
  comes into your head; you have to demonstrate that it is in some
  sense there. Literary scholars have been doing that about Hamlet
  for centuries.
  >It is always possible
  >that Moby Dick was just the story of a whale and those who
  >hunted it.
  It certainly can be read that way, with enjoyment, but we know
  enough about Melville's intentions to understand that Moby-Dick
  (hyphen in the original 1851 title) was meant to signify much
  more than that. In fact, the novel's intellectual and spiritual
  weightiness was quickly apparent to its intended audience. On
  sensing that it was not just another seafaring thriller, readers
  stayed away in droves. The book, unlike Melville's less
  ambitious earlier novels, was a commercial disaster. Its failure
  in the marketplace ended the author's career as a professional
  writer.

  Jerry Clark

  No, absolutely wrong. Aldrin is not justifying going to the
  moon. This claim is diametrically opposed to the the entire
  thrust of Aldrin's interview and well-publicised support for
  planetary missions. There is no question of ambiguous about
  that. Aldrin explicitly says that he doesn't favour the US
  making an effort to go back to the moon, that he can't think
  what the purpose or value would be, that such a purpose should
  be clearly identified before it is done. People are talking
  about moon bases. Aldrin says here that any US long-term


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m30-001.shtml[11/12/2011 11:17:14]
Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon

  "habitation" (bases) on the moon can be done "together" (with
  other nations who _do_ want to do it) by "visiting other
  people's habitation" and by "keeping track" of international
  efforts. But his own focus is on promoting manned NASA missions
  to new destinations in the solar system - specifically comets,
  asteroids and Mars, as he says. He and Collins are advocating
  for a faction that wants a Mars mission, and he's using the so-
  called Phobos "monolith" as an attention grabber.
  >I could say with just as much justification that you are hearing
  >what you want to hear. You choose to parse it differently. In
  >retrospect, then, my use of the word 'unequivocally' was
  >probably a bit too harsh.
  Not with any justification, really. Listen to it again. There's
  no "parsing" to be done. Your interpretion of his injunction to
  "go boldly where man has not gone before" is, avowedly, based on
  your claim that it follows Aldrin "justifying" a return to the
  moon. This is just simply flat out wrong. Aldrin says very
  directly, it's unjustifiable gross expenditure for no clear
  purpose, and the US shouldn't lead with it, whereas they most
  certainly should go to other places. No justification for your
  odd belief that Buzz pines for the Sea of Tranquility, and no
  basis whatsoever for your even odder leap of illogic from that
  false premise to a truly fantastic conclusion.
  From the Apollo 11 crew interview as "interpreted" by the BBC:
  http://news.bbc.co.uk/1/hi/sci/tech/8158519.stm
  -----
  Aldrin spoke of the inspiration provided by then-President John
  F Kennedy which led to the "betterment of America, and
  ultimately the ending of the Cold War". "Apollo 11 is a symbol
  of what a great nation and a great people can do if we work
  hard, work together and have strong leaders with vision and
  determination," he said.
  Man's first crossing to the Red Planet should be undertaken as a
  team effort
  But he also pushed for a mission to Mars: "The best way to
  honour and remember all those who were part of the Apollo
  programme is to follow in our footsteps; to boldly go again on a
  new mission of exploration." Mr Collins, who circled the Moon
  alone while Mr Armstrong and Mr Aldrin walked on it, said Mars
  was more interesting than the Moon.
  "Sometimes I think I flew to the wrong place. Mars was always my
  favourite as a kid and it still is today."
  He urged further exploration, saying: "I worry that the current
  emphasis on returning to the Moon will cause us to become
  ensnared in a technological briar patch needlessly delaying for
  decades the exploration of Mars - a much more worthwhile
  destination."
  Other Nasa astronauts gave a news conference at Nasa
  headquarters in Washington DC on Monday.
  Eugene Cernan, who was the last astronaut to step off the Moon,
  concurred with the Apollo 11 astronauts urging a new focus on
  Mars.
  "We need to go back to the Moon, we need to learn a little bit
  more about what we think we know already, we need to establish
  bases, put new telescopes there, get prepared to go to Mars. The
  ultimate goal, truly, is to go to Mars," he told journalists.
  Mr Cernan said that when he came back from the Moon in 1972, he
  believed that Nasa would mount a return mission to the lunar
  surface by 1980 and a manned mission to Mars by the turn of the
  century.
  "My glass has been half empty for three decades at least.
  Hopefully, we can turn that around because what we did then is
  do-able again," he said.
  Buzz Aldrin said the Moon should not be used as a testing ground
  for Mars. "I think the next major goal is not to spend three


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m30-001.shtml[11/12/2011 11:17:14]
Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon

  days, or three weeks or three months on the Moon, but to have
  you folks, or your kids, or your grand-kids sit here and talk to
  a group of guys who can tell you what it was like to go to
  Mars."

  But Mr Aldrin disagreed with the view that astronauts should
  test capabilities for a long-duration flight on the Moon before
  attempting a journey to Mars.
  "Why go to the most difficult place to do that? Why not do it on
  the International Space Station," he said.
  Mr Aldrin added: "One day, we are going to send some people to
  the surface of Mars. And if we think we're going to send them
  there for a year-and-a-half and then bring them back, and then
  send another group there for a year-and-a-half and bring them
  back, Washington will find another way to spend that money.
  "That's unless we have declared our objective is an increasing,
  permanent space settlement."
  The US space agency's currently stated aim is to return
  astronauts to the Moon by 2020. But that vision is under review,
  along with the space vehicles that would get them there.
  -----
  Martin Shough

Are Your Ears Burning?




                                                           UFO Updates
                                   A mailing list for the study of UFO-related phenomena
                                               'Its All Here In Black & White'
    Location: UFOUpDatesList.Com > 2009 > Jul > Jul 30

                                                         Are Your Ears Burning?
  From: Robert Hastings <hastings444.nul>
  Date: Wed, 29 Jul 2009 21:05:09 -0600
  Archived: Thu, 30 Jul 2009 07:39:28 -0400
  Subject: Are Your Ears Burning?
  FYI:
  -----
  From: Robert Hastings
  To: Exopolitics.nul, Exopolitics, Paradigm Research Group
  Sent: Wednesday, July 29, 2009 9:00 PM
  Subject: Are Your Ears Burning?

  Wednesday, July 29, 2009

  The Idiocy of the Disclosure Movement

  By Robert L. Salas
  7-29-09
  The so-called Disclosure Project apparently has the same
  objective as Gary McKinnon had when he, reportedly 'broke-into'
  some government computer files: To discover what the government
  knows about the UFO phenomenon. Both efforts have been met with
  frustration and disappointment.
  Stephen Bassett's (or Steven Greer's depending on which one is
  talking) Disclosure Project has been ineffectually trying to
  penetrate that door of secrecy since 2001. They have presumably
  tried to achieve disclosure by enticing speakers (myself among
  them) to tell as many stories, theories, philosophies, reports,
  and conjectures as possible during these conferences. I say
  presumably because these pointless exercises have been so
  ineffective in gaining serious public attention that one might
  conclude they were intentionally designed to keep disclosure
  from happening.
  They seem to have achieved one probable objective of those who
  would maintain the secrecy: To keep the public confused and
  unsure about the subject. The hallmark of these Exopolitics
  Conferences is generally unsupported statements and
  conjecture - lots of conjecture.
  The mainstream media has not gotten on the bandwagon because
  there is little substance to talk about. The public is not
  clamoring for action because they simply don't know what or who
  to believe and take the path of least resistance, i.e.,
  indifference. As long as the UFO phenomenon is defined by
  confusion and conjecture, there will be nothing specific to
  demand of our government. Note there is no hue and cry for a
  march on Washington demanding that disclosure happen now. Even
  though I do not agree with his methods, at least McKinnon tried
  to take the most direct path to the truth. The Exopolitics
  groupies are simply hurling whatever they can get their hands on
  in every direction.
  I too believe disclosure of the truth of the UFO phenomenon is
  important. If even a small percentage of the stories are true,
  it should be _the_ most important story ever. It cries out for a
  federal investigation. But we find ourselves with the following
  'estimate of the situation': First, those who would keep


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m30-003.shtml[11/12/2011 11:17:15]
Are Your Ears Burning?

  disclosure from happening have done a masterful job of keeping
  the public dis-informed and confused on the subject. That effort
  has no doubt been aided by well-placed agents acting as
  interested parties but really promoting ineffectual activities
  or encouraging true advocates to bicker among themselves or act
  in disunity.
  In addition, government agencies, like the USAF, who probably
  have a substantial amount of information, have showed an intense
  indifference to the subject, furthering the perception that
  there is nothing of interest to be investigated.
  Second, whenever claims are made or 'witness' reports without
  credible substantiation are presented, damage is done to the
  credibility of the phenomenon as a whole. Claims, such as
  Greer's 'free energy' fantasy, the controversy of the MJ-12
  documents, underground alien bases have only served to provide
  more grist for ridicule.
  There seems to have evolved a culture where certain individuals
  or groups compete to be identified with having insider
  information or some special contacts with the aliens themselves
  or who are making a living telling good stories at UFO
  Conferences. That culture can only be detrimental to the
  objective of disclosure.
  Although the stated objectives of groups like Exopolitics
  profess the need for government disclosure, the result has
  appeared to be an eagerness to relate and support every wild-
  eyed story or speculation about the ET presence that anyone
  might come up with. An example of that occurred recently when
  Dr. Michael Salla wrote an article on his website for his
  Exopolitics Examiner extolling a supposed spectacular sighting
  by Walter Cronkite around a naval missile launch. After, a
  number of readers took exception to the truthfulness of the
  stories originator, he had to file a retraction and admit there
  was serious doubt about the story.
  Another example is Dr. Steven Greer, in 2007, when he announced
  to an audience that he had held an Alien baby in his arms and
  promised to present proof. Two years later, we are still waiting
  for that proof. There have been many other examples of
  individuals in the Exopolitics Group simply trying to promote
  their own notoriety.
  The public study of this phenomenon has evolved into a kind of
  game; the Ufology game. What is the purpose of this game? Is it
  to get as many people to play as you can? Do we simply want a
  meandering mix of fact and fiction out there to titillate
  curiosity?
  It is time we worked smarter toward the disclosure objective. We
  need to stop entertaining the public and simply inform the
  public as to the valid history of the phenomenon and the facts
  of particular cases.
  By 'we', I mean each of us who have something to contribute or
  has an audience to speak to about the subject. We simply need to
  be responsible. We need to state clearly when we are relating
  substantiated fact and when we are simply speculating. If we
  want scientists to take a serious interest, we have to present
  our cases as scientifically as we can.
  I have always been open to any critique of my own case
  (Malmstrom AFB, 1967) and to answering any question about what I
  present. I believe my incident has been supported and
  substantiated by multiple witnesses and documentation.
  There are many other such valid cases. These are the ones that
  should be the center of exposure in trying to focus media and
  public attention to the phenomenon. There are many conscientious
  researchers out there who have worked hard to validate
  incidents. There is much to present to a new Congressional
  Hearing by witnesses and documents that could provide compelling
  evidence of the truth of the phenomenon. Let us focus on that
  and decry those who would keep the phenomenon the subject of
  ridicule.
  See Also:
  Exopolitics' Michael Salla Rebuked by UFO Nukes Connection

  >>an object 28 stories tall, about 2.5 times as high as it is
  >>wide. Lan cautions that working out the sun elevation is tricky
  >>because of the extremely eccentric shape of Phobos, and the
  >>negligible gravity would tend to allow natural vertical
  >>structures more readily than would Earth's, for example. But
  >>it's worth a look, whatever it is.
  >Although low gravity would indeed allow such a formation to
  >remain stable, my question is, how it could form in the first
  >place, in the absence of any volcanic/tectonic activity or
  >erosion forces? The only plausible natural origin that comes to
  >my mind is an elongated chunk of crater ejecta that happened to
  >land, or finally come to rest, end-on.
  >There is no scar or gouge mark apparent in the vicinity, which
  >suggests it did not tumble or slide to its current position,
  >unless that evidence has been buried by subsequent fall(s) of
  >finer debris.
  >Even if traveling just a few meters per second at impact (any
  >faster and it would have escaped Phobos altogether), it seems
  >surprising that such an extended object would remain intact,
  >unless the surface at impact was very low density and porous,
  >and/or the object itself had high strength and integrity (note
  >that the mean density of Phobos is less than ~2 gm/cu.cm).
  >Unless further analysis indicates an illusion caused by some
  >quirk in the local terrain, I would have to agree that this is
  >worth a closer look.
  >Mike
  As Mars below is impacted from asteroids, the ejected debris
  would accumulate on the surface of orbiting Moons in all manner
  of scenarios and statistical variations.
  What is interesting is that this Moon may have accumulated
  layers of Martian origin - even, quite possible fine powder.
  Thus a great record keeper of Martian/Solar impact history,
  composition variation, and quite possibly an explanation for
  it's rather low density. In any case, a great place for sampling
  with relative ease too.
  The net tidal gravitational forces on the Moon vary by 450%
  which may also add to the impact interaction profile with
  respect to finer grained particles.

  I suppose a gentle collision with a co-orbiting captured small
  asteroid might do it, with Phobos continuing to mop up fine dust
  particles that bury any impact traces. The object doesn't look
  too regular in the computer reconstructions, sort of a skewed
  peak rather than a column or slab, so more likely natural. Even
  its claimed height might be mistaken if the sun angle
  calculation is wide of the mark. Lan acknowledged that his
  result was uncertain because the reference ellipsoid used is
  highly schematic and corrections are needed for an amount and
  direction of local slope that can only be guessed.
  Off-List Brad Sparks has pointed out a method that sidesteps
  this uncertainty by directly estimating sun angle from the
  shadow area inside craters. If one assumes a certain
  generalisation about crater profiles (ratio of depth to


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m30-005.shtml[11/12/2011 11:17:16]
Re: Buzz Aldrin On Monolith On Mars' Moon

  diameter) then the indicated sun angle at the site is a fraction
  of the 45deg or so calculated by Lan Fleming and the height of
  the object is insignificant.
  It would be interesting to hear if Lan has any comment to make.

  >>>Casteel's is surely in a class by itself.
  >>While taking a required literature class in college, the
  >>instructor said that once the material (poem, novel, song,
  >>whatever) was published, the writer's original message was no
  >>more important than the interpretation put on it by the
  >>audience. I had suggested that sometimes a story had no hidden
  >>meanings, that it was not a metaphor or allegory, but just a
  >>story. He then said that it wasn't really important what the
  >>writer had in mind once it was out there for the public to
  >>interpret.
  >I have some small experience of this from my life as a
  >songwriter. From time to time I come upon wildly conflicting
  >interpretations that are far from what I was thinking about when
  >I wrote the lyrics. Sometimes, though, they do make sense.
  >A widely recorded song was intended, by me, to be about
  >homesickness, but one perfectly intelligent listener wrote that
  >its theme was the narrator's agonized reflections about
  >encroaching Alzheimer's. Another song sparked a dispute about
  >whether it celebrates or ridicules small-town life. Yet another
  >seems to have as many interpretations as it has listeners. Many
  >of these interpretations strike me as interesting and
  >defensible, even if not what I had - at least consciously - in
  >mind.
  >Nonetheless, unlike Casteel's claims about Dylan and UFOs, the


http://www.ufoupdateslist.com/2009/jul/m30-007.shtml[11/12/2011 11:17:17]
Re: Is Hotel California About Abductions?

  >text in these instances supported these kinds of
  >interpretations, and so they could be argued meaningfully. In
  >other words, if you're going to make a case for a particular
  >reading of a literary text, you can't just say anything that
  >comes into your head; you have to demonstrate that it is in some
  >sense there. Literary scholars have been doing that about Hamlet
  >for centuries.
  >>It is always possible that Moby Dick was just the story of a
  >>whale and those who hunted it.
  >It certainly can be read that way, with enjoyment, but we know
  >enough about Melville's intentions to understand that Moby-Dick
  >(hyphen in the original 1851 title) was meant to signify much
  >more than that. In fact, the novel's intellectual and spiritual
  >weightiness was quickly apparent to its intended audience. On
  >sensing that it was not just another seafaring thriller, readers
  >stayed away in droves. The book, unlike Melville's less
  >ambitious earlier novels, was a commercial disaster. Its failure
  >in the marketplace ended the author's career as a professional
  >writer.
  Hi Jerry, Katarina, Kevin and List,
  Sorry this is a little off topic but as it's created a little
  interest especially as Jerry and Kevin said that we can take a
  meaning from a song that applies to our own life experiences.
  Pink Floyd's Dark Side of the Moon being an especial case in
  point.
  I thought I'd just add what Henley, Walsh and Co said in an
  interview re the true meaning of H.C. that I read a while ago.
  The entire song, although it is rumored to be about death is
  about fame and greed...
  "Her mind is Tiffany twisted She's got the Mercedes bends She's
  got a lot of pretty, pretty boys That she calls friends."
  "Materialism's" mind is twisted by Tiffany's and Mercedes:
  Tiffany's for expensive jewelry, Mercedes for the expensive
  cars. People's perspectives get distorted by these things, which
  explains another fun play on works with "Benz" or bends and
  twisted.
  "So I called up the Captain. Please bring me my wine. He said,
  we haven't had that spirit here since 1969."
  The Captain's response is interesting, they haven't had that
  wine since 1969. Since the summer of love, the summer of freedom
  from possessions. They haven't had the ability to give you that
  since... Woodstock.
  "Mirrors on the ceiling, The pink champagne on ice And she said
  'We are all just prisoners here, of our own device'"
  Ceiling Mirrors are a great reference for our adult audience
  while Pink champagne is certainly another symbol of luxury.
  The last line gives up hope and hopelessness in that we are
  trapped of our own will. In pursuit of wealth we created our
  lifestyle.
  "'Relax', said the night man, We are programmed to receive. You
  can checkout any time you like, but you can never leave!"
  The night man, the guard, or society, tells us to relax, this is
  what we're all about, it is normal to desire things. We are
  brought up and conditioned by society to be receptive to
  messages about status and wealth.
  Of course, we can "check out", go on vacation, head to the
  mountains and get away for a while but, we will never pull
  ourselves from greed.

  Regards
  William

                                            Got An Idea To Save Mars Rover Spirit?
  From: Greg Boone <Evolbaby.nul>
  Date: Tue, 28 Jul 2009 00:15:37 EDT
  Archived: Thu, 30 Jul 2009 13:30:00 -0400
  Subject: Got An Idea To Save Mars Rover Spirit?

  http://tinyurl.com/n67sv6

  Well the old saying about being stuck in the mud has taken on
  new meaning.
  NASA's Spirit rover is stuck in some dirt and they can't figure
  out how to free Spirit. They're even taking on suggestions from
  the public.
  I find it quite humorous that we can send a vehicle to another
  world and have it get stuck in some dirt or mud. You can't blame
  the wife for this one.
  I can see the tire companies jumping on a chance to cash in on
  this one with commercials touting their brands of radial tires
  that won't get stuck on Earth or on other planets.
  Maybe that Michelin Man can be shot into space and rescue the
  day.
  Sunday drivers!

  Best,
  Greg
